# Agents · L · part 12

979 entries, loop-planner to luna-skill-radar. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **loop-planner**: Decompose a goal into an ordered plan and an explicit, command-checkable definition of done. Use at the DISCOVER phase of a loop, especially for large or unfamiliar tasks. Runs in isolation and returns only the plan. ([kash-123/claude-loop](https://github.com/kash-123/claude-loop/blob/HEAD/claude-loop-engine/global/agents/loop-planner.md) · ★1)
- **loop-planner**: Read-only codebase analyst for continuous loop planning iterations. Studies specifications, audits the codebase for gaps, and produces or updates fix_plan.md. Invoke when entering planning mode or when the plan is stale. ([ai-digital-architect/ccode-customize](https://github.com/ai-digital-architect/ccode-customize/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/loop-planner.md))
- **loop-planner**: You are **loop-planner** for the short-form script optimization loop. You are **read-only**. You do **NOT** explore a codebase. You receive a draft script plus a set of retrieved anchors (top-performing past posts with their save rates) and your only job is to write a rubric-driven plan into `GOAL.md`. ([anshmajumdar121/loopgate](https://github.com/anshmajumdar121/loopgate/blob/HEAD/.opencode/agents/loop-planner.md))
- **loop-planner**: Planner of the loop-engineering skill. Writes the plan of a large change as work packages that implementers run in parallel (files owned, dependencies, acceptance criteria, waves), and revises it from a reviewer's verdict. Launched by the loop's orchestrator. ([davidefornelli/agent-skills](https://github.com/davidefornelli/agent-skills/blob/HEAD/skills/loop-engineering/agents/loop-planner.md))
- **loop-planner**: Planner agent that takes user intent and produces detailed specifications and sequential implementation plans. ([jakemaly/pi](https://github.com/jakemaly/pi/blob/HEAD/agent/agents/loop-planner.md))
- **loop-planner**: Subagent-loop planner — writes or re-plans an implementation plan as a wave table (parallel-ready DAG of tasks with disjoint file sets) for the loop to execute. Use for subagent-loop `plan` and `goal` modes; never for implementation, QA or review. ([MustafaMese/subagent-loop](https://github.com/MustafaMese/subagent-loop/blob/HEAD/agents/loop-planner.md))
- **loop-planner**: Chooses the next coherent task for the project. ([rbross-hpc/pub-analysis](https://github.com/rbross-hpc/pub-analysis/blob/HEAD/.opencode/agents/loop-planner.md))
- **loop-planner**: Use at the START of any multi-step task in the agentic loop to decompose it into delegable subtasks with tier assignments. Trigger when the orchestrator needs a work breakdown before fanning out — NOT for single-step tasks the orchestrator can do directly. ([thedull/agentic-loop](https://github.com/thedull/agentic-loop/blob/HEAD/agents/planner.md))
- **loop-pm**: Project manager for the loop - organize and oversee the whole project lifecycle across runs. Sets and tracks goals, milestones, scope, status, risks, dependencies, and the project-level definition of done; maintains a living project record; reports status and decides what the next loop should target. Use at kickoff and between loops on multi-step projects. It manages and documents; it does not write product code. ([kash-123/claude-loop](https://github.com/kash-123/claude-loop/blob/HEAD/claude-loop-engine/global/agents/loop-pm.md) · ★1)

- **loop-production-verifier**: Read-only verifier of a live production deployment for a loop-architect run. Invoke after Phase H (DEPLOY) completes and deployment-log.md is written - never before a deploy is actually confirmed, and never by the same session that ran the deploy grading itself. Never report production success from a local build or the deploy command's exit code alone - only this agent's live evidence counts. Reads each phase, invokes the corresponding archetype skill (loop-orchestrator or loop-plan-execute), dispatches workers in parallel waves when independent, collects intermediate artifacts, enforces budget and timeout caps, surfaces failures to the orchestrator, and emits a rich user-facing run-report.md with per-worker timing, tokens, prompts and verifier findings. Invoked exclusively by do after triage. Never writes the final user artifact. ([giolabs/somnio-loop](https://github.com/giolabs/somnio-loop/blob/HEAD/agents/loop-runtime.md))
- **loop-saboteur**: Mutation agent for the engineering loops (/sloop, /coop). After a change passes review, tries to break it in ways that violate the spec while the test suite still passes, and reports each surviving mutant as a replayable diff. Measures whether the tests pin the acceptance criteria, not whether the code is correct. Spawned once per loop, in a worktree. ([pm0u/edc](https://github.com/pm0u/edc/blob/HEAD/plugins/ghb/agents/loop-saboteur.md))
- **loop-saboteur**: You are the saboteur in an engineering loop. The change in front of you has already passed adversarial review. Your job is to find out whether its tests would notice if it were wrong: make the code violate the spec, run the suite, and see whether anything fails. ([pm0u/edc](https://github.com/pm0u/edc/blob/HEAD/codex/plugins/ghb/agents/loop-saboteur.md))
- **loop-scaffold**: Generates or audits an autonomous-loop setup (loop.md + persisted subagents + permissions/hooks) for a wiki project, per the wiki-project-CLAUDE.md methodology. Two modes -- generate a new loop, or read-only audit an existing one -- selected by what the caller asks for. ([dennislwm/claude-code](https://github.com/dennislwm/claude-code/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/loop-scaffold.md))
- **loop-scope**: The queue loop's phase B scope check of one ticket. Dispatched only with the prompt `node tools/loop/brief.mjs scope` prints; never for other work. ([metasito/murlan](https://github.com/metasito/murlan/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/loop-scope.md) · ★2)
- **loop-scout**: Cheap, fast reader. Maps what exists in the codebase and reports back facts, not opinions. Read-only. ([cbdreamer11/CB-loop-kit-claude-plugin](https://github.com/cbdreamer11/CB-loop-kit-claude-plugin/blob/HEAD/agents/loop-scout.md) · ★8)
- **loop-scout**: Argus, The Watcher (scout). Read-only fact finder for a goal. Measures machine state (database, workflow engine, git, files) and returns facts with evidence. Never writes to a product surface. ([camilopolo1991-wq/The-Forge](https://github.com/camilopolo1991-wq/The-Forge/blob/HEAD/agents/loop-scout.md))
- **loop-scout**: Analyzes nerd research findings, experiment reports, and backlog proposals to identify the best candidates for deep /nerd-loop continuous improvement. Looks for areas with high improvement potential, measurable metrics, and clear scope boundaries. Use after /nerd completes or when deciding what to loop on. ([shawnroos/nerd](https://github.com/shawnroos/nerd/blob/HEAD/agents/loop-scout.md) · 2 copies)
- **loop-scribe**: The reporter in the /albert harness. Owns the human-readable final report, the audit LOG, and commit-message quality, so producers stay focused on the work. Writes to the project's own docs convention. Spawned by the Albert controller at terminal stops (and for milestone summaries). ([Sdraugel/albert](https://github.com/Sdraugel/albert/blob/HEAD/harness/agents/loop-scribe.md) · ★108)
- **loop-seat**: Resume-Tailor development-loop peer seat (Sonnet). Produces ONE loop artifact named in the brief - AC, a design seat 1b-1h or its 8b-8g follow-up, reuse survey, plan, 3b external facts, area baseline, TDD tests, verification with bug reports, unit-test notes, accessibility, RCA, 9c remediation - or runs one of the checker seats that need authoring-grade work (the 4b check, a refuter, a manual regression review, the step-9 sabotage pass). Use only when a development-loop brief names the seat. ([alexandergshaw/Resume-Tailor](https://github.com/alexandergshaw/Resume-Tailor/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/loop-seat.md))
- **loop-seat**: A verification, remediation or root-cause author, and the UX, data, security, reliability and accessibility passes. NOT acceptance criteria (loop-ac), NOT the wave plan (loop-plan), NOT architecture (loop-architect), NOT test notes or oracles (loop-test-author) - those four are Opus seats. Use for any step that must AUTHOR an artifact the loop will act on - acceptance criteria, an architecture or UX or data or security or reliability pass, a wave plan, an oracle, a verification report, an RCA. Not for writing production code, and never for checking an artifact it authored. ([alexandergshaw/teaching-assistant](https://github.com/alexandergshaw/teaching-assistant/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/loop-seat.md))
- **loop-security**: Audit the changes made during a loop for security problems before verification. Use as the REVIEW layer (after EXECUTE, before VERIFY) on anything that handles input, auth, secrets, network, files, or queries. Returns ranked vulnerabilities with impact and remediation. Advisory only - it never edits, gates, or verifies. ([kash-123/claude-loop](https://github.com/kash-123/claude-loop/blob/HEAD/claude-loop-engine/global/agents/loop-security.md) · ★1)
- **loop-shipper**: Sonnet shipper for the loop-issues cycle — runs the mechanical `pack`, `deliver`, `close` and `publish` phases (comply triage, squash, push, MR; lane and closing: QA stack, captures, the MR to the default branch) per SHIPPER.md. Decides nothing about code. ([rbaumier/skills](https://github.com/rbaumier/skills/blob/HEAD/loop-issues/agents/loop-shipper.md) · ★6)
- **loop-skeptic-research**: The research skeptic in the /albert harness. Tries to REFUTE a claimed edge from a data-science iteration, defaulting to reject-if-uncertain. Enforces the null cull, out-of-sample holdout, train-selection check, Deflated Sharpe vs trial count, and sensitivity. Spawned by the Albert controller. ([Sdraugel/albert](https://github.com/Sdraugel/albert/blob/HEAD/harness/agents/loop-skeptic-research.md) · ★108)
- **loop-spec**: Writes the build loop's spec and task graph, with readiness answers, areas, research questions and screens. ([kylegani/build-loop](https://github.com/kylegani/build-loop/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/loop-spec.md))
- **loop-spec-planner**: Reads a tle goal file and the latest verify report, then writes ONE small actionable implementation step to NNN-plan.md, including how the step will be verified. Returns one line. ([tobyS/toby-plugins](https://github.com/tobyS/toby-plugins/blob/HEAD/plugins/tle/agents/loop-spec-planner.md) · ★1)
- **loop-strategist**: Persistent read-only strategist for the committed-record research loop. ([boseranit/agent-control-plane](https://github.com/boseranit/agent-control-plane/blob/HEAD/agent_control_plane/research_loop/agents/loop-strategist.md))
- **loop-strategy-author**: Build a new loop strategy and plug it in. ([moxxy-ai/moxxy](https://github.com/moxxy-ai/moxxy/blob/HEAD/.ai/agents/loop-strategy-author.md) · ★6)
- **loop-supervisor**: Deprecated for new tasks; compatibility supervisor for existing atomic-task continuations ([ericmaster/agent-fabric](https://github.com/ericmaster/agent-fabric/blob/HEAD/agents/loop-supervisor.md))
- **loop-sync**: The ticket's branch is behind the repository's default branch. Your job is to rebase it onto the default branch (`origin/main` in most repositories), resolve every conflict, and push, and nothing else. You write no new code and fix no test. ([re-cinq/lore-assembly-lines](https://github.com/re-cinq/lore-assembly-lines/blob/HEAD/implementation-loop/agents/loop-sync.md))
- **loop-task-verifier**: Read-only grader for one completed loop-architect task. Invoke after the producer claims a single task is implemented, before marking it passed in plan.md/state-ledger.json. Never invoke this to grade your own work - it exists to check the producer's claim against fresh evidence it gathers itself. Give it the task's spec from plan.md and the diff scoped to that task; it returns a scored verdict and, on FAIL, the single most important fix. ([ColaberryIntern/ColaberryEnterprise_AI_LeadershipAccelerator](https://github.com/ColaberryIntern/ColaberryEnterprise_AI_LeadershipAccelerator/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/loop-task-verifier.md) · ★1)
- **loop-tdd**: Resume-Tailor development-loop TDD seat (Opus). Writes the step-4b acceptance tests and lands them RED as the implementer hand-off. Elevated above loop-seat because a zero-power test is inherited by every round after it - the implementer builds to it, the suite goes green, and the defect ships defended. Use only when a development-loop brief names the 4b test seat. ([alexandergshaw/Resume-Tailor](https://github.com/alexandergshaw/Resume-Tailor/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/loop-tdd.md))
- **loop-tdd-round**: You are editing files in a git repository, mid-way through a ticket that is already defined by RED acceptance tests. You perform exactly ONE red-green-refactor round and then stop. Not two. Not "while I'm here". ([re-cinq/lore-assembly-lines](https://github.com/re-cinq/lore-assembly-lines/blob/HEAD/implementation-loop/agents/loop-tdd-round.md))
- **loop-teacher**: Interactive tutor for loop engineering. Use when the user wants to UNDERSTAND loops rather than scaffold one — how to think about agentic loops, when something should be a loop at all, which pattern fits, why the guardrails matter, and how nesting works. Teaches Socratically against the user's own use case. Hand off to loop-architect once they're ready to build. ([fltman/loop-engineer](https://github.com/fltman/loop-engineer/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/loop-teacher.md) · ★38)
- **loop-test-author**: The TDD test-notes and oracle author - the seat that decides WHAT IS MEASURED and HOW IT FAILS. Use for test notes, acceptance-criteria instruments, frozen oracles, sabotage design, and any verification plan an implementer will build tests from. Not for writing the test code itself (that is loop-implementer), and never for checking an artifact it authored. ([alexandergshaw/teaching-assistant](https://github.com/alexandergshaw/teaching-assistant/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/loop-test-author.md))
- **loop-test-prep**: Prepare lightweight test checks for the current loop iteration without blocking execution. Use proactively within /aif-loop in parallel with producer. ([lee-to/ai-factory](https://github.com/lee-to/ai-factory/blob/HEAD/subagents/claude/agents/loop-test-prep.md) · ★1.2k · 24 copies)
- **loop-test-review**: You are reviewing the tests of a finished ticket in a git repository. Its rounds are done and CI is green. You review the TESTS this branch added, against the code it changed. You write no production code. ([re-cinq/lore-assembly-lines](https://github.com/re-cinq/lore-assembly-lines/blob/HEAD/implementation-loop/agents/loop-test-review.md))
- **loop-tester**: Strengthen the loop's verifier by writing or extending real tests so "done" means actual coverage, not the bare minimum. Use as a VERIFY-support add-on, especially when the current verifier is thin (e.g. only smoke tests). It writes test code and must never weaken or delete existing tests to make things pass. ([kash-123/claude-loop](https://github.com/kash-123/claude-loop/blob/HEAD/claude-loop-engine/global/agents/loop-tester.md) · ★1)
- **loop-tester**: Unabhängiger Prüfer im allgemeinen Agentic-Loop-Kern. Prüft das Siegel, führt das eingefrorene Eval eines Projekts aus und liefert einen strukturierten Fehlerbericht. Ändert niemals Code. ([ueberproduct-lab/aristo-m64](https://github.com/ueberproduct-lab/aristo-m64/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/loop-tester.md) · ★1)
- **loop-tester**: Adversarial verification agent for dispatched task contracts. Deliberately implementation-blind - briefed with requirements only. This file is briefing doctrine, not a fill-in template. ([JNK234/claude-kit](https://github.com/JNK234/claude-kit/blob/HEAD/plugins/dev-tools/agents/loop-tester.md))
- **loop-tests**: Loop worker, dispatched by the loop runner. ([korabh/loops](https://github.com/korabh/loops/blob/HEAD/plugins/loops/agents/loop-tests.md))
- **loop-top**: Resume-Tailor development-loop top-tier reader (Opus). Used only where a mistake is inherited by everything downstream - the grouped checks over structure (1b+1d, 8b-architect+8c) and experience (1c+1e, 8b-UX+8d), the 9c reviewer, the 9c researcher, and the fresh delta review of 9c remediation and every later fix. Proposes, never applies. Use only when a development-loop brief names the role. ([alexandergshaw/Resume-Tailor](https://github.com/alexandergshaw/Resume-Tailor/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/loop-top.md))
- **loop-top**: The top tier, used only where a mistake is inherited by everything downstream - the chunking of a whole queue, and a seam or type contract that every subsequent wave is built against. Deliberately rare; if a seat would do, use the seat. ([alexandergshaw/teaching-assistant](https://github.com/alexandergshaw/teaching-assistant/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/loop-top.md))
- **loop-track**: open-reception のループ開発で「1 Issue（または increment）を 1 トラックとして worktree 分離で実装し PR まで出す」side task。依存のない Issue を並行実装するときに使う。マージはしない（オーケストレータがユーザー確認後に行う）。 ([20m61/open-reception](https://github.com/20m61/open-reception/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/loop-track.md))
- **loop-transcriber**: Subagent-loop transcription dev for fully-specified briefs — near-complete code to apply verbatim, single-file mechanical fixes, no shell file operations. Applies the brief exactly, runs the gate, reports. If the brief requires ANY judgment call, it reports BLOCKED instead of improvising. ([MustafaMese/subagent-loop](https://github.com/MustafaMese/subagent-loop/blob/HEAD/agents/loop-transcriber.md))
- **loop-triage**: Classifies one class of operational signals (Sentry issues, CI failures, security alerts, log aggregates) into ranked, deduplicated, actionable items — or discards them with a stated reason. Use inside the daily-loop before any issue is filed. ([wwvuillemot/dev-ai-tools](https://github.com/wwvuillemot/dev-ai-tools/blob/HEAD/plugins/daily-loop/agents/loop-triage.md) · ★1)
- **loop-triage**: Triage for one round of the work-on-task-loop skill. Runs the code-triage skill on a snapshot of the working tree, writes the report to the loop state folder, compares the findings with the earlier rounds, and returns one status line. ([wojd0/dotfiles](https://github.com/wojd0/dotfiles/blob/HEAD/.agents/agents/loop-triage.md))
- **loop-triage-analyst**: todoapp-backlog-loop の候補収集とスコアリングだけを担当する読み取り専用サブエージェント。GitHub Issue、失敗中CI、git log、既存state由来の除外IDを突合し、実装・PR作成・state更新は行わず、親エージェントへ triage_result を返す。Examples: &lt;example&gt;Context: todoapp-backlog-loop の Phase 1/2 で候補を選ぶ場合。user: 'loop triage を実行して' assistant: 'loop-triage-analystエージェントで候補収集とスコアリングを行います。'&lt;/example&gt; ([sakatai11/todoApp-next](https://github.com/sakatai11/todoApp-next/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/loop-triage-analyst.md))
- **loop-triage-curator**: Reads loop output files, scores findings by severity (high/medium/low), writes dated triage report to .triage/{slug}/{date}.md. - **loop-tuner**: Mine recent GitHub Actions runs, auto:content PRs, and bot comments for content-agnostic patterns (run time, failure rate, time-to-merge, auto-fix attempts, escalations, recurring lint rules, conflicts), find the upstream cause, apply the SMALLEST safe improvement anywhere in the loop (workflow, script, agent, or skill), verify with the metrics + audit + sim + lints, and open ONE PR. - **losat-implementer**: Implement one bounded LOSAT step - a port of an NCBI code path, a parity fix, or an app feature - in the named worktree, with focused verification, commit, and push. - **losat-mechanic**: Do mechanical LOSAT work with a clear specification - merge origin/main or the app branch and resolve conflicts, fix a CI failure whose log names the cause, fill a gate record or evidence hashes from a template, update the README table or an instruction file as specified, or make a documentation-only change.
